A grown-up-sized long-sleeved V-neck bodysuit in denim, with a high-cut leotard-style bottom with finished leg openings, sewn entirely from real fabric as a wearable version of the outfit of a vaudeville performer. The upper body is made as a striped blazer with a straw boater, in knit jersey with satin trims, every collar, lapel, strap and pocket a separate sewn piece. white trousers forms the bottom half in brushed flannel, stitched seamlessly into the top. An attached hood at the neck is sewn as a straw boater in knit jersey. A fabric version of cane is stitched onto the suit. The garment is assembled from cut panels of fabric joined with visible seams, topstitching and piping, and it hangs as one coherent piece with every part in the outfit's own cloth. The uniform fills the whole straight-sided garment shape edge to edge, the same width at chest, waist and hem. A full vaudeville performer costume, head to toe in a single suit, with a striped blazer with a straw boater, white trousers on the legs, a straw boater (as the hood), spats (as the feet), a cane sewn or attached to the suit. Nothing of the outfit is missing and the legs of the suit show the lower garment. Novelty embellishments, knitted rib panels with raised cable texture, metallic foil-print accents that catch the light and oversized novelty buttons in contrasting colours. Everything is chosen to be fun to look at and tactile to feel, bumpy, fluffy, shiny and soft in turn.

The top half is built as a patterned suit, cut from boucle and minky plush in the outfit's colours, with a real sewn collar, real pockets, buttons or fastenings, and trims stitched on. An attached hood at the neck is sewn as a mask in boucle. The suit is built from layered fabric pieces sewn together, colour-blocked in the outfit's colours, with finished edges and neat stitching throughout. The outfit is laid out on the garment's own rectangle shape, fully covering it from side seam to side seam. A full shapeshifting hero costume, head to toe in a single suit, with a skin-tight patterned suit, a mask (as the hood), boots (as the feet). Nothing of the outfit is missing and the legs of the suit show the lower garment. Novelty embellishments, tufted boucle accents, glow-in-the-dark stitched details and layered ruffle cape-like shoulders. It is a novelty costume garment, made to be bright, playful, over-the-top and a pleasure to touch.

An attached hood at the neck is sewn as ribbons in brushed flannel. A fabric version of microphone is stitched onto the suit. Every part is real fabric, panels cut, stitched together and finished with seam binding, with the outfit's details made as sewn pieces. The outfit is laid out on the garment's own rectangle shape, fully covering it from side seam to side seam. A full idol singer on stage costume, head to toe in a single suit, with a frilled stage dress, ribbons (as the hood), boots (as the feet), a microphone sewn or attached to the suit. Nothing of the outfit is missing and the legs of the suit show the lower garment. Novelty embellishments, velvety flocked appliqué shapes, pocket flaps and straps added just for fun and fluffy sherpa patches at the elbows and knees. It is a novelty costume garment, made to be bright, playful, over-the-top and a pleasure to touch.

The outfit is stretched to the garment's own straight-sided shape and fills it completely, so it suits any body size. An attached hood at the neck is sewn as a mask in velour. Every part is real fabric, panels cut, stitched together and finished with seam binding, with the outfit's details made as sewn pieces. The outfit is stretched to the garment's own straight-sided shape and fills it completely, so it suits any body size. It is a complete costume of speed hero in one piece, shown full length from collar to ankles, with a yellow and blue bodysuit with swirling speed lines, a mask (as the hood), boots (as the feet). The upper half carries the top and the leg tubes carry the lower garment, so the whole outfit reads at once. Novelty embellishments, chunky pom-pom trim along the hood edge and cuffs, puffy padded shoulder pieces and terry-loop textured stripes. It is a novelty costume garment, made to be bright, playful, over-the-top and a pleasure to touch.
A straight-cut adult-sized long-sleeved crew-neck bodysuit in vinyl-look fabric, with a classic leotard cut with smooth finished leg openings, sewn entirely from real fabric as a wearable version of the outfit of a glam rock star. The top half is built as a sequined jumpsuit, cut from minky plush and denim in the outfit's colours, with a real sewn collar, real pockets, buttons or fastenings, and trims stitched on. An attached hood at the neck is sewn as big hair in minky plush. A fabric version of guitar is stitched onto the suit. The outfit is laid out on the garment's own rectangle shape, fully covering it from side seam to side seam. The suit is the whole glam rock star outfit, not just a top, with a sequined jumpsuit, big hair (as the hood), platform boots (as the feet), a guitar sewn or attached to the suit. It is shown in full, from the neckline down to the ankle cuffs. Novelty embellishments, gold-tone and silver-tone zipper pulls shaped like charms, pocket flaps and straps added just for fun and velvety flocked appliqué shapes. Everything is chosen to be fun to look at and tactile to feel, bumpy, fluffy, shiny and soft in turn.

The top half is built as an ice-white gown with fur, cut from faux leather and boucle in the outfit's colours, with a real sewn collar, real pockets, buttons or fastenings, and trims stitched on. An attached hood at the neck is sewn as a frost crown in faux leather. The suit is built from layered fabric pieces sewn together, colour-blocked in the outfit's colours, with finished edges and neat stitching throughout. The outfit is stretched to the garment's own straight-sided shape and fills it completely, so it suits any body size. A full winter queen costume, head to toe in a single suit, with an ice-white gown with fur, a frost crown (as the hood). Nothing of the outfit is missing and the legs of the suit show the lower garment. Novelty embellishments, quilted puffy panels on the chest, soft chenille patches and puffy padded shoulder pieces. The embellishments are sewn on as real raised fabric, so the suit has depth, texture and sparkle from every angle.
